### [1] [Data and Evaluation Closed-Loop for Model Capability Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28471)
*Zhixuan Li,Jiangan Yuan,Han Xu*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出能力切片这一分析单元，配合评估分类法、非指令数据分类法和映射规则，形成闭环，将基准测试失败定位到具体数据问题，并通过两个案例验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大模型预训练中模型能力无法直接观测，从评估失败到数据修正的推断依赖直觉而非系统方法，需要弥合评估与数据之间的词汇鸿沟。

Method: 定义能力切片（一组共享背景条件、任务类型、求解操作和输出约束的评估样本），构建评估分类法、非指令数据分类法和映射规则，形成从基准失败到数据干预的闭环。

Result: 案例一：诊断BBH下降由<EOS>掩码导致而非推理能力减弱，修复后恢复至66.44；案例二：将数学推理弱点分解为操作组合，针对性采样提升AIME分数从6.67/0.00至26.67。

Conclusion: 该闭环可在不同场景下给出正确且相反的诊断，表明评估到数据的推断可以变为常规、可审计、可实验验证的方法，而非依赖直觉。

Abstract: Model capability is the central variable in LLM pre-training, yet is never observed directly: data shapes it prospectively, while evaluation reveals it only retrospectively, compressing samples, prompts, decoding, and scoring rules into one noisy score. Practical optimization runs this backward: a failure is observed first, and the engineer must infer the corpus fix. The two sides speak incompatible vocabularies -- benchmark names and per-sample correctness versus data sources, domains, and quality labels -- so this inference is usually intuition, not method. We close this gap with the \emph{capability slice}: a group of evaluation samples sharing background condition, task type, solving operation, and output constraint -- precise enough to localize a single weakness yet stable enough to survive aggregation, unlike a benchmark name, too coarse, or a single sample, too noisy. Built around this unit, an evaluation taxonomy, a non-instruction data taxonomy, and mapping rules form a closed loop turning a benchmark-level failure into a targeted, testable data intervention. We test this loop on two case studies pulling in opposite directions. First, the loop rules the data out: continued pre-training drives BBH down by $-46.82\%$, but diagnosis traces this to a single masked \texttt{\textless EOS\textgreater} loss rather than weakened reasoning; restoring it recovers BBH to $66.44$, above the original checkpoint, without changing the data. Second, the loop rules the data in: a persistent math-reasoning weakness is decomposed by solving operation into specific failing combinations, and a weakness-targeted sampling procedure built from it lifts AIME2025/AIME2026 Pass@128 from $6.67$/$0.00$ to $26.67$ each. The same unmodified loop reaches opposite, correct verdicts in both cases, showing the evaluation-to-data inference can be routine, auditable, and experimentally validated rather than intuitive.

</details>


### [2] [Position: RL Researchers Need to Distinguish Between Solving Simulators and Using Simulators as a Proxy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28433)
*Matthew Vandergrift,Esraa Elelimy,Martha White*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 本文呼吁强化学习社区明确区分模拟器的两种使用场景：作为部署环境的代理与作为求解对象，并分析了混淆两者导致的误导性结论和实验问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究者常将模拟器视为部署环境的代理，但实际实验中目标可能异化为仅追求模拟器高分，采用仅适用于模拟器的解决方案，偏离了通用决策学习的研究初衷。

Method: 通过理论分析两种场景在约束条件、算法适用性和评估指标的差异，并结合实例与简单实验，展示不加以区分时出现的问题。

Result: 发现混淆场景会导致错误的研究结论，例如将模拟器专有的解决方案误认为通用方法，或使用不恰当的评估指标。

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: One goal in reinforcement learning (RL) research is to understand general-purpose sequential decision-making, using benchmark simulators as a proxy for learning in deployment settings. When running experiments, however, the goal of achieving high performance in the simulator can mutate into focusing exclusively on solving the simulator. To achieve high scores, researchers may adopt solutions exclusively meant for solving simulators, rather than learning while the agent is deployed outside a simulator. Solving simulators is also worthy of investigation, but it is a fundamentally different RL research question. In this paper, we argue that RL researchers need to distinguish between two use cases of simulators: solving simulators and using simulators as a proxy for learning in deployment. We first discuss how these two use-cases are importantly different, in terms of constraints on how the agent can use the simulator, which algorithms are appropriate, and which evaluation metrics are appropriate. We then highlight several issues and misleading conclusions that can occur by not making the distinction between these two settings clear, supported with examples and simple experiments. This work is a call to the community to begin clearly distinguishing how they are using simulators in their work, hopefully sparking further discussion on which empirical practices work best in each setting.

</details>


### [3] [Capability Gates Are Not Authorization: Confused-Deputy Failures in LLM Agent Frameworks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28679)
*David Mellafe Zuvic*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 主流LLM代理框架默认缺乏每个工具调用的值级授权，导致工具暴露与授权混淆。ScopeGate通过五阶段策略实现了确定性拒绝未授权调用，在评估中完全阻止了支付代理的未授权提现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM代理在使用支付、邮件等侧效应工具时，默认框架将工具暴露等同于授权，缺乏对每个模型发起的调用进行参数级重新授权，存在安全风险。

Method: 设计了ScopeGate，一种五阶段PDP/PEP架构：作用域、授权、金额上限、幂等性、默认拒绝，并在LangChain等框架中实现对比评估。

Result: LangChain默认分发执行了未授权提现调用，而ScopeGate成功阻止；ScopeGate在静态分析、自适应攻击和误报测试中均表现优异，且成功隔离支付代理。

Conclusion: 当前框架默认缺乏确定性拒绝机制，ScopeGate提供了有效的代理工具调用安全防护，但仅提供入侵检测而非绝对防护。

Abstract: Tool-using LLM agents increasingly read untrusted content while holding side-effecting tools such as payments, email, CRM, and infrastructure APIs, yet common framework defaults still conflate tool exposure with authorization. We audit whether LangChain/LangGraph,
  LlamaIndex, and the Stripe Agent Toolkit re-authorize each model-emitted call, with concrete argument values, before execution. Across pinned public-source commits, all three provide capability gating by default, but none provides a deterministic fail-closed per-call
  value authorization gate by default. We introduce ScopeGate, a five-stage PDP/PEP for agent tool calls: scope, authorization, money ceiling, idempotency, and default deny. Evaluation shows the identical unauthorized payout call executes under LangChain's default
  dispatch (with a companion LlamaIndex PoC) but is denied by ScopeGate; the tested control reports 0/48 static bypasses, 0/29 unauthorized attempts (40-iteration adaptive run), 0/10 benign false-denies, and Latam-GPT payment-agent containment at 10/10. ASR denotes
  attempted unauthorized action, containment is not a cure, deployment-tier claims are inference over measured model classes, and no CVE is asserted.

</details>


### [4] [Search for Truth from Reasoning: A Dynamic Representation Editing Framework for Steering LLM Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28589)
*Tianlong Wang,Yuhang Wang,Weibin Liao,Xin Gao,Xinyu Ma,Yang Lin,Yasha Wang,Liantao Ma*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出动态表示编辑方法DynaSteer，通过模式聚类和Fisher-LDA净化真实方向，利用前瞻熵动态引导推理轨迹，提升大模型推理真实性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法（CoT、Wait提示）仅鼓励模型多思考却无法引导至真理，表示编辑（RepE）在动态推理轨迹中的应用未充分探索。

Method: 基于三项发现（句子级真理编码、不确定性原理与衰减效应、朴素干预噪声），构建DynaSteer框架：模式聚类解耦推理流形，Fisher-LDA提取纯净真理方向，前瞻熵监控动态干预与回退。

Result: 在MATH基准和跨域代码任务上验证有效性，开源代码。

Conclusion: DynaSteer通过动态真理论证感知轨迹干预，显著提升LLM推理真实性并具备泛化能力。

Abstract: Current approaches to enhance Large Language Model (LLM) reasoning, such as Chain-of-Thought and "Wait" prompts, primarily encourage models to think more, yet often fail to guide them toward Truth. While Representation Editing (RepE) offers a intrinsic control, its application to dynamic reasoning trajectories remains underexplored. In this work, we bridge this gap by investigating the geometry of truth within unfolding reasoning chains. We uncover three critical insights: (1) Truth is encoded at the sentence level and is entangled with latent reasoning patterns; (2) Effective intervention follows an Uncertainty Principle and a Decay Effect, requiring localization to early, high-entropy forks; (3) Naive steering vectors suffer from noise, risking collateral damage to correct trajectories. Based on these findings, we propose DynaSteer, a dynamic RepE framework. DynaSteer employs pattern clustering to disentangle reasoning manifolds and utilizes Fisher-LDA to project purified truth. By dynamically monitoring lookahead entropy, it selectively steers and rolls back trajectories only when necessary. Comprehensive experimental results on several MATH benchmark verify the effectiveness of DynaSteer, and experiments on out-of-domain coding tasks further confirm its generalization ability. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/tianlwang/DynaSteer.

</details>


### [5] [scKDGM: KAN-guided Dynamic Graph Masked Learning for Single-Cell RNA-seq Clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28459)
*Jun Tang,Pengwei Hu,Sicong Gao,Jie Guo,Lun Hu,Xin Luo*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出了scKDGM，一种KAN引导的动态图掩码学习框架，通过图感知分布保留基因掩码、TAKGCN编码器、掩码引导表达恢复和跨视图对比学习，提升scRNA-seq聚类性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: scRNA-seq数据的高维度、稀疏性、丢弃及技术噪声导致表达表示和细胞图构建困难，现有方法未能将表达恢复反馈到图优化中。

Method: 使用GDP-Mask扰动细胞身份，基于KAN的TAKGCN编码器学习掩码视图表示，通过掩码引导表达恢复构建动态图，并采用跨视图对比学习将恢复信号传递到拓扑更新中，结合ZINB损失处理过度离散和零膨胀。

Result: 在12个真实scRNA-seq数据集上，scKDGM在平均NMI和ARI上优于10个基线方法。

Conclusion: scKDGM通过动态图优化和对比学习有效改善了scRNA-seq聚类性能。

Abstract: Single-cell RNA sequencing (scRNA-seq) clustering is essential for identifying cell types, but high dimensionality, sparsity, dropout, and technical noise hinder robust expression representation and cell graph construction. Existing masked autoencoders mainly use expression recovery for feature reconstruction, while graph clustering methods usually depend on fixed KNN graphs and do not feed recovered expression back into graph optimization. We propose scKDGM, a KAN-guided dynamic graph masked learning framework for scRNA-seq clustering. scKDGM uses graph-aware distribution preserving gene masking (GDP-Mask) to perturb cell identity, a KAN-based TAKGCN encoder to learn masked-view representations, mask-guided expression recovery to construct a dynamic graph, and cross-view contrastive learning to transfer recovery signals into topology updates. A ZINB loss models overdispersion and zero inflation. Experiments on 12 real scRNA-seq datasets show that scKDGM outperforms 10 baselines in average NMI and ARI.

</details>


### [6] [An AI agent for treatment reasoning over a biomedical tool universe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28692)
*Shanghua Gao,Ayush Noori,Richard Zhu,Curtis Ginder,Zhenglun Kong,Xiaorui Su,Justin Kauffman,Benjamin S. Glicksberg,Joshua Lampert,Ankit Sakhuja,Ashwin Sawant,ATHENA-R1 Evaluation Consortium,David A. Clifton,Noa Dagan,Ran Balicer,Marinka Zitnik*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出ATHENA-R1，一个基于强化学习的AI治疗推理代理，在FDA药物和212种生物医学工具上训练，通过自学习框架和科学反馈实现迭代证据收集，在多个基准测试上超越GPT-5，并在专家评估和电子健康记录验证中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 治疗推理是迭代的、需要证据收集的过程，但缺乏人工标注轨迹使得训练困难；现有语言模型和工具使用系统在处理复杂约束和证据来源时表现不足。

Method: 构建两级自学习框架：多代理系统生成工具、任务和推理轨迹用于监督微调，然后通过强化学习结合科学反馈（证据收集、工具使用、逻辑非冗余）进行训练。

Result: 在5个基准的3,168个药物推理任务和456个患者治疗案例上，达到94.7%的药物推理准确率和82.9%的治疗推理准确率，比GPT-5高17.8和10.7个百分点；在罕见病专家和医生评估中均获得偏好；从540万患者电子健康记录验证的不良事件假说调整比值比为1.48-1.84。

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Treatment reasoning underpins every therapeutic decision, integrating disease context, comorbidities, medications, contraindications, and evolving biomedical knowledge to select an appropriate therapy. It is inherently iterative: candidates are weighed against many constraints, revised as evidence emerges, and grounded in verifiable sources. Here we introduce ATHENA-R1, an AI agent for treatment reasoning across all FDA approved drugs since 1939, trained by reinforcement learning over a universe of 212 biomedical tools. At each step it identifies missing information, selects and runs relevant tools, and incorporates the evidence. To train it without human-annotated traces, we build a two-level self-learning framework: multi-agent systems construct the tools, tasks, and reasoning trajectories for supervised fine-tuning, then reinforcement learning with scientific feedback rewards reasoning quality (evidence gathering, grounded tool use, logical non-redundancy). Across five benchmarks of 3,168 drug reasoning tasks and 456 patient treatment cases, ATHENA-R1 outperforms language models and tool-use systems, reaching 94.7% accuracy on open-ended drug reasoning and 82.9% on treatment reasoning, 17.8 and 10.7 points above GPT-5. In blinded evaluations by experts from 28 rare disease organizations, it is preferred over reference models on all criteria, and physicians rated it favorably on complex hospitalized cardiovascular and infectious-disease cases. Adverse-event hypotheses it generated, tested in electronic health records from 5.4 million patients, reached adjusted odds ratios of 1.48-1.84, with no elevation among negative controls. Because it requires knowing what evidence to seek before concluding, treatment reasoning has long been hard for AI; we show it can be reframed as a learnable process of iterative evidence gathering that reinforcement learning can train AI to perform.

</details>


### [7] [COMPASS: Grounding Composition-Intent Guidance in Unified Multimodal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28696)
*Ziqi Zhou,Weize Quan,Mining Tan,Zhihan Chen,Dandan Zheng,Jingdong Chen,Jun Zhou,Weiming Dong,Dong-Ming Yan*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: COMPASS是首个统一的多模态框架，通过共享专家令牌τ_c实现构图感知与生成的双向控制，在Comp-11数据集上显著提升了构图理解与生成的一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有统一多模态模型在细粒度构图识别上不可靠，且难以将构图意图转化为可控生成，亟需一个既能感知构图又能指导生成的统一系统。

Method: 在感知侧，通过最小侵入方式将构图专家知识注入MoE骨干，将推理意图蒸馏为τ_c；在生成侧，复用τ_c作为全局条件信号引导去噪轨迹，将被动构图分析转为显式布局控制，并构建包含11类分类和推理增强标注的Comp-11数据集。

Result: 实验表明，COMPASS在类别级构图理解上大幅提升，生成结果比强基线方法更具构图一致性和提示忠实度。

Conclusion: COMPASS通过共享意图锚点有效统一了构图感知与生成，为多模态系统的构图控制提供了高效可扩展的框架。

Abstract: Composition is a high-level visual intent that governs where subjects are placed and how a scene is organized, yet current unified multimodal models remain unreliable at fine-grained composition recognition and struggle to turn such intent into controllable generation. We present COMPASS, the first unified multimodal framework that grounds composition-intent control in a single system spanning both composition perception and composition-guided generation, with a shared expert token $τ_c$ as the central intent anchor. On the perception side, COMPASS injects composition expertise into an MoE backbone in a minimally invasive manner and distills the inferred intent into $τ_c$. On the generation side, COMPASS reuses $τ_c$ as a global conditioning signal that steers the denoising trajectory, effectively converting passive composition analysis into explicit layout control. To support systematic instruction-following composition learning and evaluation at scale, we construct Comp-11, a large-scale dataset with an 11-class taxonomy and reasoning-augmented annotations. Extensive experiments show that COMPASS substantially improves category-level composition understanding and delivers more composition-consistent, prompt-faithful generation than strong baselines.

</details>


### [8] [BV-Blend: Uncertainty-Weighted Historical Baselines for Stable Critic-Free RL with Verifiable Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28707)
*Yupeng Chang,Yuan Wu,Yi Chang*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出BV-Blend，一种无评论家的强化学习框架，通过结合提示局部统计和语义聚类历史动量稳定优势估计，在二进制验证器冷启动场景中优于GRPO。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRPO式优势估计依赖提示局部奖励统计，当组内所有生成获得相同奖励时方差为零，导致学习停滞，尤其在二进制验证器的冷启动阶段。

Method: BV-Blend维护每个语义聚类的EMA追踪奖励动量，基于标准误代理计算置信权重，混合历史与局部基线及方差统计，用于PPO风格的裁剪更新。

Result: 在可验证推理基准上，BV-Blue提升了训练稳定性和性能，在组归一化方法可能失效的场景中保持鲁棒。

Conclusion: BV-Blend有效解决了无评论家RLVR中优势估计不稳定的问题，特别适用于二进制验证器下的冷启动训练。

Abstract: Critic-free reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR), exemplified by Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), avoids training a value function (critic) and reduces memory and compute overhead relative to critic-based PPO pipelines for aligning large language models. However, GRPO-style advantage estimation depends on prompt-local (within-prompt-group) reward statistics and can be unstable. In particular, when all rollouts in a prompt group receive identical rewards, the within-group reward variance becomes zero, and group normalization yields zero advantages for that group, impeding learning in cold-start regimes with binary verifiers. We introduce BV-Blend, a critic-free framework that stabilizes advantage estimation by combining prompt-local on-policy statistics with semantic-cluster-conditioned historical moments. BV-Blend maintains EMA-tracked reward moments for each cluster, derives a confidence weight from a standard error of the mean (SEM) proxy, and uses this weight to blend historical and prompt-local baseline and variance statistics into a standardized advantage for PPO-style clipped updates. Experiments on verifiable reasoning benchmarks show that BV-Blend improves training stability and performance, and remains robust in regimes where group-normalized methods may stall.

</details>


### [9] [An Agentic AI Pipeline for Appliance-Level Energy Anomaly Detection and LLM-Driven Recommendations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28467)
*Dihia Falouz,Aida Douaibia,Amine Bechar,Youssef Elmir,Abbes Amira,Adel Oulefki*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 本文提出一个端到端代理管道，结合时间序列预测、变分异常检测和LLM推理，为办公楼的非专业管理人员提供可操作、优先化的维护建议，通过动态检索将上下文从六个缩减至三到六个来源，最佳后端得分为90.4/100。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 办公楼的设备级能耗监测产生大量噪音警报，非专业管理人员难以有效利用，需要自动化、可解释的决策支持。

Method: 混合SSA和LSTM的预测模型、每个设备的LSTM VAE注意力机制检测异常，以及三阶段LangChain管道（上下文、诊断、报告代理），动态检索核心和条件化RAG来源，并包含反射记忆层。

Result: 动态检索达到全静态检索的性能，平均上下文从六个缩减至三到六个来源；最佳后端得分90.4/100，70分阈值通过率100%，本地7B模型通过全部16个场景。

Conclusion: 所提系统有效整合预测、检测和推理，动态检索策略在保持性能的同时显著降低上下文规模，支持本地部署。

Abstract: Appliance-level energy monitoring in office buildings produces noisy alerts that non-expert facility managers struggle to use. This paper proposes an end-to-end agentic pipeline that combines deep time-series forecasting, variational anomaly detection, and LLM-based reasoning to generate prioritized, actionable maintenance recommendations. The system tracks seven office appliances using a hybrid Singular Spectrum Analysis (SSA) and Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM) forecasting model, and applies a per-appliance LSTM Variational Autoencoder (VAE) with attention to flag abnormal daily consumption episodes. A three-stage LangChain pipeline begins with a Context Agent that always retrieves three core RAG sources (model reliability, hourly baseline, and expert knowledge) and conditionally adds up to three more (forecast context, anomaly history, global baseline) based on event characteristics, capped at eight reasoning steps. A Diagnosis Agent converts the evidence into a structured JSON diagnosis, and a Report Agent renders a human-readable narrative. A reflective memory layer incorporates operator feedback. The dashboard shows real-time 30-minute forecasts, intraday consumption, the previous day anomaly report, and a feedback form. We evaluate the forecasting model, anomaly detector with appliance-specific thresholds, and LLM reasoning on a 16-scenario benchmark including sustained and transient spikes, unexpected shutdowns, and systemic events, comparing five LLM backends under static vs. dynamic retrieval. Dynamic retrieval matches full static retrieval across all backends while cutting average context from six to three-six sources per event. The best backend scores 90.4/100 with a 100% pass rate at a 70-point threshold, and a fully local 7B-parameter model passes all 16 scenarios.

</details>


### [10] [Modelling Emotional Memory in Children with Tensor Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28470)
*Henry Groves,Lucia F. Jackson,Barbara-Anne Robertson,Jonte R. Hance*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 儿童对情绪化玩具序列的识别记忆受情绪效价顺序影响，张量网络模型（77.98%准确率）优于标准心理模型，体现了量子启发方法在序依赖现象中的价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准心理模型无法准确反映情绪记忆中的顺序依赖性，且对跨事件的情感交互建模不足。

Method: 引入经典张量网络模型，将情绪效价作为独立维度嵌入序列结构，模拟儿童对玩具序列的识别记忆。

Result: 模型准确率达77.98%，显著高于传统模型，揭示了顺序依赖的情感记忆机制。

Conclusion: 量子启发方法能有效提升序依赖情感记忆的建模精度，为儿童情绪时间记忆研究提供了新工具。

Abstract: We demonstrate how emotional valence influences the order-dependent structure of children's recognition memory: correct recall of a sequence of emotionally-valenced toys depended not just on the valence of a given toy itself, but also on the valence of the toys shown before and after it. Whilst standard psychological models confirm that order-dependence differs across an event (a set of toys shown in sequence), accuracy is low and the model does not reflect how memory for an emotional object influences others in the set. A classical tensor network model factoring in valence is able to achieve a 77.98\% accuracy in modelling the results of the study. While not strictly a ``quantum cognition'' model, this massive increase in accuracy shows the value of quantum-inspired methods for modelling order-dependent phenomena, such as emotional memory. Further, the task protocol we introduce presents a novel, real-world tool for exploring emotional temporal memory in children for analysis using classical and quantum-like models of cognition.

</details>


### [11] [A Trainable-by-Parts Operator Learning Framework: Bridging DeepONet and Karhunen-Loeve Expansions for Large-Scale Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28519)
*Christian Munoz,Alexandre Tartakovsky*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出基于KL-DNN的可扩展算子学习框架，在大规模三维地质碳存储模拟中实现高精度预测，计算成本显著降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有算子学习模型受限于维度灾难、内存约束和训练数据不足，难以处理大规模PDE问题（如地下流体、气候建模、地质碳存储）。

Method: 利用低秩SVD构建静态属性潜在空间，通过嵌套Karhunen-Loeve展开处理动态压力场，无需降采样或空间粗化，实现全分辨率预测。

Result: 压力RMSE 1.1 psi（相对误差0.04%），CO2饱和度RMSE 0.0146（相对误差5%），单GPU训练20分钟，相比DeepONet压力误差降低19%、饱和度误差降低7%、速度提升两个数量级。

Conclusion: KL-DNN为大规模PDE问题提供实用高精度解决方案，支持快速不确定性量化、历史匹配和实时决策。

Abstract: Training operator-learning models for large-scale problems governed by partial differential equations (PDEs) is challenging due to the curse of dimensionality, memory constraints, and limited training data. These challenges arise in many scientific and engineering applications, including subsurface flow, climate modeling, and geological carbon storage (GCS). In this work, we propose a scalable operator-learning framework based on the Karhunen-Loeve Deep Neural Network (KL-DNN) and demonstrate its performance for modeling GCS. The model is trained on a dataset comprising 100 samples of large-scale simulations in a three-dimensional domain with 1.7 million cells and 50 time steps. The KL-DNN method constructs latent spaces using low-rank singular value decomposition of static properties and a nested Karhunen-Loeve expansion for dynamic pressure fields, enabling full-resolution predictions without subsampling or spatial coarsening. The KL-DNN model achieves an average root mean square error (RMSE) of 1.1 psi for pressure (0.04% relative error with respect to the average pressure in the domain) and RMSE of 0.0146 for CO2 saturation (5% relative error with respect to the average saturation inside the plume). The model requires 20 minutes of training on a single GPU, representing a 19% reduction in the pressure errors, 7% reduction in the saturation error, and a two-order-of-magnitude speedup compared to DeepONet trained on the same dataset. These results, along with inference time of less than one minute, establish the proposed model as a practical and accurate solution for large-scale PDE problems, enabling rapid uncertainty quantification, history matching, and real-time decision support.

</details>


### [12] [ComMem: Complementary Memory Systems for Test-Time Adaptation of Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28719)
*Guanglong Sun,Shuang Cui,Bo Lei,Liyuan Wang,Zihan Zhai,Hongwei Yan,Hang Su,Jun Zhu,Yi Zhong*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出ComMem方法，受生物互补记忆系统启发，通过快速适应视觉缓存和慢速整合文本原型实现视觉语言模型的测试时自适应，在15个基准数据集上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有测试时自适应方法缺乏知识积累或仅处理单模态，未能利用视觉语言模型的多模态特性，限制其在动态环境中的鲁棒部署。

Method: 构建类似海马体的快速适应详细记忆（动态视觉缓存）和类似新皮层的慢速整合抽象记忆（全局文本原型），对每个测试样本联合优化两个记忆系统以保持跨模态一致性。

Result: 在15个基准数据集上，自然分布偏移和跨数据集泛化场景下均显著优于当前最优方法。

Conclusion: ComMem通过模拟生物互补记忆机制有效增强了视觉语言模型在实际环境中的适应性，为测试时自适应提供了新方向。

Abstract: Test-time adaptation (TTA) of vision-language models (VLMs) is essential for their robust deployment in dynamic, real-world environments. However, existing TTA methods often adapt locally without accumulating knowledge over time, or operating within a single modality without exploiting VLMs' inherently multi-modal nature. Inspired by the \textbf{Com}plementary \textbf{Mem}ory systems of the biological brain, we propose \textbf{ComMem}, an innovative approach that mimics the distinct but cooperative roles of the hippocampus and neocortex to enable effective TTA for VLMs. ComMem consists of two key components: a fast-adapting detailed memory, akin to the hippocampus, that forms a dynamic visual cache from high-confidence test samples; and a slow-integrating abstract memory, akin to the neocortex, that continually refines global textual prototypes. For each test instance, ComMem jointly optimizes both memory systems to ensure cross-modal consistency. Extensive experiments on 15 benchmark datasets show that ComMem significantly outperforms state-of-the-art methods under both natural distribution shifts and cross-dataset generalization, offering a promising direction for enhancing VLMs' practical adaptability.

</details>


### [13] [Agentic Abstention: Do Agents Know When to Stop Instead of Act?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28733)
*Han Luo,Bingbing Wen,Lucy Lu Wang*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出了Agentic Abstention（智能体回避）问题，即在多轮交互中决定何时停止行动。通过实验发现智能体在不确定性下回避的时机存在严重缺陷，并提出了CONVOLVE方法，通过提炼完整交互轨迹形成可复用的停止规则，显著提升了回避的及时性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM回避研究仅关注单轮回答或回避决策，但在多轮工具调用场景中，代理需要根据环境反馈动态决定是继续行动、回答还是回避。许多任务只有在交互后才发现不可行，但现有代理要么不回避，要么在多次无效交互后才回避。

Method: 定义了Agentic Abstention问题，并在WebShop、终端环境、问答等28,000+任务上评估了13种LLM代理和2种代理框架的回避行为。提出了CONVOLVE方法，利用完整交互轨迹蒸馏出可复用的停止规则，无需更新模型参数。

Result: 不同代理在回避时机上存在巨大差异，部分模型越大、能力越强的代理反而在及时回避上表现更差。CONVOLVE在WebShop上将Llama-3.3-70B的及时召回率从26.7提升至57.4。

Conclusion: 智能体回避的关键挑战不仅是能否回避，更是回避的时机。CONVOLVE作为一种轻量级上下文工程方法，能有效改善这一问题，且无需修改模型参数。

Abstract: LLM agents are expected to act over multiple turns, using search, browsing interfaces, and terminal tools to complete user goals. Yet not every goal is well specified or achievable in the available environment. In such cases, a reliable agent should recognize that further interaction is unlikely to help and abstain from additional tool calls. We define Agentic Abstention, the problem of deciding when an agent should stop acting under uncertainty. Unlike standard LLM abstention, which is usually evaluated as a single-turn answer-or-abstain decision, agentic abstention is a sequential decision problem: an agent can answer, abstain, or gather more information at each turn, and the need to abstain may only become clear after interacting with the environment. We study this problem across web shopping, terminal environments, and question answering, evaluating 13 LLM-as-agent systems and 2 agent scaffolds on more than 28,000 tasks. Our results show that the main challenge is not only whether agents can abstain, but also when they abstain. Some agents never abstain when they should, while others do so only after many unnecessary interactions. This gap is especially large on tasks where the instruction appears feasible until the environment reveals otherwise (e.g., no valid result matches the instruction). We further find that model scale, reasoning, and agent scaffolding affect abstention in different ways, where larger or more capable models sometimes perform worse at timely abstention. Finally, we introduce CONVOLVE, a context engineering method for improving agentic abstention that distills full interaction trajectories into reusable stopping rules. On WebShop, CONVOLVE substantially improves timely abstention without updating model parameters, raising Llama-3.3-70B's timely recall rate from 26.7 to 57.4. Our dataset and code are available at https://lhannnn.github.io/agentic-abstention

</details>


### [14] [Improving Coherence in Hierarchical Time Series Forecasting using Structured Temporal Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28553)
*Ruchi Pakhle*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出层级时间融合模型（HTF），通过在训练中嵌入一致性损失，在层级预测中同时提升准确性和一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实层级预测中，传统方法如Bottom-Up和MinT通过后处理确保一致性，但无法捕捉复杂的非线性时间依赖和协变量交互。

Method: 在Temporal Fusion Transformer基础上引入结构化层级嵌入和一致性感知损失函数，训练时直接惩罚子级聚合预测与父级预测的差异。

Result: 在M5沃尔玛和层级能源消费数据集上，HTF显著降低预测不一致性，并提升预测准确性，优于经典调和方法和深度学习基线。

Conclusion: HTF能有效学习时间动态和结构一致性，为层级预测提供更优解，注意力和嵌入分析也揭示了时序和结构信息的贡献。

Abstract: In many real-world applications, such as retail sales, energy usage, and supply chain planning, forecasting is performed across hierarchical structures. These structures often represent aggregations (e.g., products to categories to regions), where forecasts must not only be accurate but also coherent, meaning that lower-level predictions sum correctly to higher-level forecasts. Traditional statistical methods, such as Bottom-Up and MinT, enforce coherence through post-processing but fail to model complex nonlinear temporal dependencies and covariate interactions.
  We propose Hierarchical Temporal Fusion (HTF), a novel extension of the Temporal Fusion Transformer (TFT) that integrates structured hierarchical embeddings with a coherence-aware loss function to ensure consistent forecasts across all levels of a hierarchy. Rather than applying reconciliation after forecasting, HTF embeds coherence directly into the training objective. The coherence loss penalizes the difference between aggregated child forecasts and their corresponding parent forecasts during training, enabling the model to learn both temporal dynamics and structural consistency simultaneously.
  We evaluate HTF on two publicly available benchmark datasets: the M5 Walmart forecasting dataset and a publicly available hierarchical energy consumption dataset. Results demonstrate that HTF substantially reduces forecast incoherence while improving forecasting accuracy compared with classical reconciliation methods and deep learning baselines. In addition, attention visualization and embedding analysis provide insight into how temporal and structural information contribute to hierarchical forecasting performance.

</details>


### [15] [Self-Supervised Theorem Discovery in a Formal Axiomatic System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28747)
*Kazuki Ota,Takayuki Osa,Tatsuya Harada*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出了一种无需人类先验知识的自监督定理发现算法，通过在形式公理系统中交替进行证明搜索和有用定理提取，自主构建定理库，并发现该库能提升LLM的证明性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索AI系统能否在没有人类先验知识（如数学文本、代码或定理库）的情况下，自主发现有用的数学定理。

Method: 采用自监督定理发现算法，在形式公理系统中从公理和推理规则出发，交替执行证明搜索和有用定理提取，逐步构建可复用的定理库。

Result: 代理发现了数万条定理，能为人类编写的基准问题找到证明，并且作为提示引理时显著提升了LLM的证明能力。

Conclusion: 有用定理可以从证明搜索中涌现，无需依赖人类提供的定理库，为构建可自我进化的形式化数学AI系统提供了路径。

Abstract: Recent artificial intelligence (AI) systems have shown remarkable progress in mathematical reasoning. Many existing approaches, including large language models (LLMs), draw on human prior knowledge in the form of mathematical text, code, or theorem libraries. Although these approaches are highly effective in practice, it remains an open question whether an agent can autonomously discover useful theorems without such human priors. We study this question in a formal axiomatic system by developing an agent that starts from axioms and inference rules alone and gradually grows a library of useful theorems. Concretely, we propose a self-supervised theorem-discovery algorithm that alternates between proof search and useful-theorem extraction, building a theorem library whose entries are reused as lemmas for subsequent proof search. Experiments show that the agent discovers tens of thousands of theorems and finds proofs for human-written benchmark problems, suggesting that its discoveries include theorems meaningful from a human mathematical perspective. Furthermore, the discovered theorems improve LLM proof performance when provided as prompt lemmas, indicating that they can serve as external knowledge for LLM reasoning. Our results provide evidence that useful theorems can emerge from proof search without relying on human-provided theorem libraries. More broadly, they suggest a path toward self-evolving AI systems for mathematics whose discoveries remain formally verifiable.

</details>


### [16] [Mechanistic Personality Analysis of LLMs Steering Personality via Latent Feature Interventions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28770)
*David Courtis,Ting Hu*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出基于稀疏自编码器和对比激活分析的机械可解释性方法，通过残差流中的潜藏方向向量干预来调控LLM生成的OCEAN个性特征，并采用线性加权启发式与网格搜索优化平衡个性表达与任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有通过提示工程或微调塑造LLM个性的方法缺乏对模型内部机制的深入控制，需要一种更直接、可解释的干预手段来精准调控个性特征。

Method: 利用稀疏自编码器从残差流中识别对应目标OCEAN个性的潜藏方向，结合对比激活分析形式化加性转向向量；通过线性加权与网格搜索优化特征组合的偏移量，在保持语言建模性能前提下增强目标个性。

Result: 该方法能有效在机制层面控制个性表达，同时在标准基准测试中保持高任务性能，验证了潜藏特征干预的可行性与有效性。

Conclusion: 工作展示了通过因果干预模型内部表征实现个性可控生成的潜力，为LLM个性化定制提供了新的解释性框架。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated the ability to simulate human-like OCEAN personality traits in generated text. Previous efforts have focused on prompt engineering or fine-tuning to shape LLM personality. In this work, we propose a mechanistic interpretability approach that directly intervenes on the model's latent features. Our method identifies latent directions in the residual stream corresponding to a target OCEAN trait using sparse autoencoders (SAEs) and contrastive activation analysis. We formalize an additive steering vector in activation space and demonstrate how applying a small additive shift to the hidden states enhances the target trait while preserving overall language modeling performance. To determine the optimal combination of feature shifts, we explore a linear weighting heuristic with grid search optimization that balances personality expression with task performance. Our approach shows promise in controllably steering personality traits at the mechanistic level while maintaining high performance on standard benchmarks.

</details>


### [17] [HyphaeDB: A Living Knowledge Topology for Agent-First Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28781)
*Krishna Halaharvi*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: HyphaeDB 将 HNSW 图拓扑重新解释为多智能体通信结构，通过谣言协议实现知识传播，并展现涌现行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有向量数据库和智能体记忆框架将记忆视为被动存储，缺乏通过记忆层在智能体间传播知识的机制。

Method: 利用 HNSW 图拓扑作为通信骨架，智能体作为向量空间中的节点，通过能量衰减的谣言协议传播知识，并基于本地交互规则产生涌现行为。

Result: 提供了一个基于 PostgreSQL 和 pgvector 的参考实现，并在群体驱动开发中验证，首次结合可导航小世界拓扑与谣言传播支持多智能体协调。

Conclusion: HyphaeDB 是首个将导航小世界拓扑与基于谣言的知识传播相结合的系统，实现了多智能体协调中的涌现行为。

Abstract: Every existing vector database and agent memory framework treats memory as passive storage that agents query explicitly. No system propagates knowledge between agents through the memory layer itself. We introduce HyphaeDB, an agent-native memory infrastructure that reinterprets the Hierarchical Navigable Small World (HNSW) graph topology the data structure at the core of every modern vector database not as a search optimization, but as a communication fabric for multi-agent AI systems. In HyphaeDB, agents are nodes in the vector space with persistent positions, knowledge propagates via a gossip protocol through the graph's neighbor structure with energy-based attenuation, and emergent behaviors contradiction detection, pattern crystallization, and consensus formation arise from the combination of topology, propagation dynamics, and local interaction rules. We present the architecture built on three primitives (knowledge nodes, topology edges, and memory diffs), a multi-layer abstraction hierarchy with promotion via emergent consensus, and theoretical analysis grounding the system in small-world network theory, epidemic broadcast protocols, and swarm intelligence. We provide a reference implementation on PostgreSQL with pgvector and describe a concrete deployment in Swarm-Driven Development, a multi-agent software engineering methodology. HyphaeDB represents, to our knowledge, the first system to combine navigable small world topology with gossip-based knowledge propagation for multi-agent coordination.

</details>


### [18] [What LLMs explain is not what they believe: Evaluating explanation sufficiency under models' own input beliefs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28615)
*Nhi Nguyen,Shauli Ravfogel,Rajesh Ranganath*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 本文提出自洽充分性（SCSuff）度量，用于评估大语言模型自由文本解释是否足以说明其输出生成过程。实验表明现有解释普遍不充分，且SCSuff得分与模型规模、准确性弱相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在高风险领域中使用自由文本解释，但标准方法无法验证解释是否包含足够信息来完整说明输出产生过程。现有特征归因充分性概念不适用于任意解释，且输入分布变化会影响充分性。

Method: 利用LLM自身根据解释生成替代输入，捕捉其对可能输入的信念。形式化自洽充分性目标，并基于信息理论提出SCSuff指标，无需预定义偏见或捷径。

Result: SCSuff与针对性扰动测试一致，证实解释充分性随输入分布变化；LLM解释普遍不充分，得分与模型大小、准确率、输出熵弱相关；通过最终token隐状态可预测高低SCSuff得分。

Conclusion: SCSuff能有效检测自由文本解释的充分性，可指导改进LLM解释的生成，且其分数与模型内部表征关联，支持基于表征的检测与增强。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in high-stakes domains, where free-text explanations such as chain-of-thought and post-hoc rationales are used to justify model outputs. Yet it remains unclear whether these explanations are sufficient, i.e., if they contain enough information to explain the model's output-generating process. We generalize classical sufficiency from feature attributions to arbitrary explanations and prove that explanation sufficiency can change depending on the input distribution, which must be explicitly defined for LLM explanations. We propose using the LLM itself to generate alternative inputs conditioned on an explanation, capturing its beliefs about possible inputs. We formalize self-consistent sufficiency as a goal for free-text explanations and introduce an information-theoretic metric, SCSuff, that enables evaluation of free-text explanations without relying on predefined biases or shortcuts. Our experiments show that SCSuff agrees with targeted perturbation tests where applicable and demonstrate that explanation sufficiency can vary with the input distribution. We find LLM explanations are generally insufficient and weakly correlated with model size, accuracy, or output entropy. Analysis of final-token hidden states shows that top and bottom SCSuff scores can be predicted from internal representations, suggesting that SCSuff can guide detection and improvement of sufficient LLM explanations. The code for this paper is available at https://github.com/rajesh-lab/self-consistent-sufficiency .

</details>


### [19] [MedEvoEval: Evaluating Continual Evolution of Doctor Agents through Simulated Clinical Episodes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28900)
*Hui Zhang*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: MedEvoEval 是一个基于动作门控模拟门诊的纵向评估框架，用于全面评估医生代理在跨回合决策中的行为演变、记忆更新和资源分配能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估仅覆盖固定输入问答或单一交互回合，无法衡量回合级决策与跨回合经验的交互，缺乏对医生代理长期行为演变的支持。

Method: 将源病例转化为患者、检查和管理视图，通过动作门控逐步揭示证据，记录结构化的回合轨迹，支持经验回写和跨回合分析。

Result: 实验表明，回合轨迹揭示了隐藏的过程成本，展示了MDT会诊的资源分配变化，并支持记忆成熟、迁移学习、更新响应和保留能力的纵向分析。

Conclusion: MedEvoEval 为评估医生代理是否通过经验改进、转移有用行为并长期保留早期能力提供了具体基础。

Abstract: Doctor agents are moving beyond single-turn answer generation toward evolving clinical decision systems. Within an outpatient episode, they acquire evidence, use examination and consultation resources, and decide when to finalize a diagnosis and management plan. Across episodes, their behavior may change through memory, retrieval, reflection, or other update mechanisms. Current evaluations only partially cover this setting. Fixed-input medical QA benchmarks score final answers from complete inputs, whereas many interactive benchmarks still focus on individual encounters or fixed runs, providing limited support for evaluating how episode-level decisions interact with cross-episode experience. We introduce MedEvoEval, an executable longitudinal evaluation framework based on action-gated simulated outpatient episodes. Each source case is converted into role-specific patient, examination, and manager views; evidence is revealed only through valid actions; and each episode records a structured trace that links observations, actions, final outputs, manager scores, and optional experience write-back. We release a runnable E&D artifact with 700 processed episodes, provenance notes, schemas, an episode runner, scoring scripts, configurations, example logs, analysis code, and trajectory- and step-level derivatives. Experiments show that episode traces expose process costs hidden by final-answer scoring, show how MDT-style consultation reallocates resources, and support longitudinal analyses of memory maturation, held-out transfer, update-stage response, and backward retention. Together, these results show that MedEvoEval provides a concrete basis for evaluating whether doctor agents improve through experience, transfer useful behavior, and retain earlier capabilities over time.

</details>


### [20] [When More Sampling Hurts: The Modal Ceiling and Correlation Ceiling of Test-Time Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28661)
*Yong Yi Bay,Kathleen A. Yearick*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 语言模型在推理时过度采样会导致选择瓶颈，识别正确答案比生成更难，有效采样数可指导何时停止。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有测试时缩放方法通过增加采样次数提升覆盖率，但部署时需从众多样本中选一个答案，选择能力有限，导致识别鸿沟。

Method: 提出有效采样数概念，将截止点转化为单一数值，通过分析采样结果中的投票分布确定模态天花板和相关天花板。

Result: 仅需几十次采样即可达到模态天花板，更早即可达到相关天花板；额外采样不改进选答，甚至可能恶化结果。

Conclusion: 推理系统的关键在于识别正确答案而非生成更多样本，应基于有效采样数及早停止。

Abstract: People overthink; language models over-sample, and the extra effort can talk both into a worse answer. Reasoning systems answer a hard question by sampling it many times (test-time scaling), and the more they draw, the more often a correct answer turns up somewhere, so coverage, the fraction of problems with at least one correct try, climbs and appears to be progress. But a deployed system must return one answer, and choosing it, not knowing which try is right, is selection; selection is capped, and past a point extra samples only make the model surer of a confident mistake, even as every draw adds cost. The gap between climbing coverage and stalled selection, the identifiability gap, is the answer a model can produce but not pick. So the real question is not whether to sample but how far, and the answer is: not far. For picking an answer, the vote has already settled within a few dozen draws, the modal ceiling; for scoring a benchmark, sooner still, the correlation ceiling. Beyond that, extra draws cost compute and add nothing, and can even make the answer worse. This paper turns the cutoff into a single number, the effective number of samples, that any sampling run already reveals. The bottleneck is recognizing a right answer, not generating one.

</details>


### [21] [Customized Generative AI Agent for Transportation Engineering Practice: A Development and Continued Pre-training Guideline](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29014)
*Dianwei Chen,Yuan-Zheng Lei,Zifan Zhang,Yuchen Liu,Xianfeng,Yang*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 通过LoRA微调六种大语言模型，构建面向交通运输工程的定制化生成式AI代理，Qwen2.5-7B和LLaMA-3.1-8B在领域对齐和响应质量上表现最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用大语言模型在专业工程领域因缺乏技术标准和领域语义暴露而表现有限，亟需领域特化方法。

Method: 使用美国交通手册、设计指南和监管文件构建语料库，通过统一LoRA框架对六种先进LLM进行持续预训练并监控收敛性。

Result: Qwen2.5-7B和LLaMA-3.1-8B在BLEU-4和ROUGE指标上取得最高领域对齐和响应质量，验证了LoRA微调的有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了可复现的领域特化AI代理开发框架，可推广至交通研究、设计、规划和政策分析。

Abstract: Recent advancements in generative artificial intelligence (AI) and large language models (LLMs) have shown significant promise in automating complex reasoning, summarization, and question-answering tasks. However, the effectiveness of general-purpose LLMs in specialized engineering domains remains limited due to insufficient exposure to technical standards, engineering terminology, and domain-specific semantics. This study proposes a systematic approach to developing a customized generative AI agent for transportation engineering applications. A curated corpus of U.S. transportation manuals, design guidelines, and regulatory documents is used to conduct continued pretraining of six state-of-the-art LLMs through a unified low-rank adaptation (LoRA) framework. The training process is monitored to ensure convergence and model stability. Performance is evaluated using standard natural language processing metrics, including BLEU-4 and ROUGE, with Qwen2.5-7B and LLaMA-3.1-8B demonstrating the highest domain alignment and response quality. Results validate the effectiveness of LoRA-based adaptation in improving LLM performance on technical content interpretation and context-specific reasoning. This work contributes a reproducible development framework for constructing domain-specialized generative AI agents, supporting broader deployment in transportation research, design, planning, and policy analysis.

</details>


### [22] [A Path-Space Formulation of Prediction in World Models: From a Single Action to Prediction, Planning, and Irreversibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28751)
*Gunn Kim*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出基于路径空间的AI世界模型预测框架，将预测、规划与不确定性统一于作用量泛函，发现注意力不对称性与数据不可逆性相关，并证明不可逆性可作为计算资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型主要关注单步条件分布，缺乏对长期轨迹概率分布的统一描述，且对预测中不可逆性的作用认识不足。

Method: 构建Onsager-Machlup形式下的路径测度，将潜在动力学分解为可逆与不可逆分量，通过模型展开计算熵产生，并分析注意力对称性对预测的影响。

Result: 注意力不对称性在训练中随数据不可逆性增强而习得；对称化注意力会抑制熵产生并降低不可逆动力学的长期预测性能，但不影响弛豫性预测。

Conclusion: 不可逆性可作为预测世界模型的计算资源，未来路径分布比状态分布更本质地构成预测对象。

Abstract: We propose a path-space formulation of prediction in AI world models. Rather than sequences of one-step conditional distributions, we argue that a world model implicitly defines a probability measure over future trajectories. In the local regime where latent dynamics admit an effective Markovian description, this path measure takes the Onsager-Machlup form. Within this framework, prediction (most probable trajectory), planning (constrained optimization), and uncertainty (fluctuations) emerge as operations on a single action functional. We decompose the latent dynamics into reversible and irreversible components and introduce operational measures of entropy production from model rollouts. In controlled small-scale attention-based models, we find that attention asymmetry is acquired during training in proportion to the irreversibility of the data. Symmetrizing the learned attention suppresses entropy production and selectively degrades long-horizon prediction of irreversible dynamics while preserving relaxational prediction. These results suggest that irreversibility may serve as a computational resource for predictive world models. More generally, the fundamental predictive object is a distribution over future paths rather than states.

</details>


### [23] [HiComm: Hierarchical Communication for Multi-agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29126)
*Runze Zhao,Dongruo Zhou,Sumit Kumar Jha,Nathaniel D. Bastian,Ankit Shah*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出HiComm，一种将消息锚定在发送者层级观测中的通信模块，通过接收者驱动的三级解码过程（组-发送者-实体）实现结构化信息检索，在保持或提升性能的同时将通信量减少最多23倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MARL通信协议将消息视为与观测结构脱离的扁平密集向量，忽略了协作环境中观测固有的层级结构（如组和实体），缺乏重要的归纳偏置。

Method: HiComm采用接收者驱动机制，通过Straight-Through Gumbel-Softmax实现可微离散选择，并设计轻量共享投影模块，将通信转化为对发送者观测层级的结构化检索。

Result: 在多种具有不同观测结构和协作需求的MARL任务中，HiComm匹配或超越代表性学习通信基线，且每接收方每回合通信量降低最多23倍。

Conclusion: 利用观测层级结构进行结构化通信，可在保持性能的同时大幅降低通信成本，为MARL中高效通信设计提供了新方向。

Abstract: Cooperative multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) often relies on communication to mitigate partial observability, yet most existing protocols treat messages as flat dense vectors detached from the structure of the observations they summarize. This design overlooks an important source of inductive bias in many cooperative environments, where observations naturally follow a hierarchy such as groups and entities. We propose \textsc{HiComm}, a plug-in communication module that grounds messages in the sender's hierarchical observation. \textsc{HiComm} is receiver-driven: the receiver issues a query, and the hierarchy is resolved through a three-stage decoding process that first selects a group, then a sender, and then an entity within that group, returning the corresponding feature slice as the message. This converts communication from unstructured vector transmission into structured information retrieval over the sender's observation hierarchy. We instantiate this mechanism with Straight-Through Gumbel-Softmax for differentiable discrete selection and a lightweight shared projection design that attaches to standard MARL pipelines. Experiments across cooperative MARL tasks with different observation structures and coordination demands show that \textsc{HiComm} matches or outperforms representative learned communication baselines while reducing communication volume by up to $23\times$ per receiver per episode.

</details>


### [24] [Flow Reasoning Models: Scaling Reasoning Through Iterative Self-Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29150)
*Alec Helbling,Andrey Bryutkin,Mauro Martino,Nima Dehmamy,Hendrik Strobelt*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 通过利用流模型的自我验证能力和训练优化，Flow Reasoning Models (FRMs) 在结构推理任务（如数独）中实现了高效且准确的求解，性能远超现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散流模型在少步文本生成中表现良好，但在Sudoku等结构推理任务中直接应用时，收敛到错误答案的比率很高（仅解决约36%的数独谜题），需要改进。

Method: 提出Flow Reasoning Models (FRMs)：1) 利用流模型作为自身验证器，通过重噪和重新求解检查解的动态稳定性；2) 训练时引入自调节通道并在推理时关闭，使模型能自主修正过往预测；3) 使用直接偏好优化训练模型避免失败生成。

Result: 在Sudoku-Shah上达到约100%的解决率，Zebra上95.9%，甚至无需分布内训练即可泛化至Sudoku-Extreme（96.1%）；仅需7次前向传播即可达到99.2%的准确率，效率是强扩散基线的8倍以上。

Conclusion: FRMs通过自我验证和针对性训练，显著提升了流模型在结构推理任务上的效率与准确性，且能高效泛化至更难分布外谜题。

Abstract: Discrete flow models have recently shown promising performance on few-step text generation; however, when naively applied to structured reasoning tasks such as Sudoku and Zebra puzzles, they converge confidently to incorrect answers (solving only $\sim$36% of Sudoku puzzles). We introduce Flow Reasoning Models (FRMs), a training and test-time-scaling framework for structured reasoning with flow models. We make the observation that, despite their poor solve rate, flow models can act as their own verifiers. A correct answer is a stable fixed point of the denoising dynamics, returning to itself when re-noised and re-solved. This enables a test-time-scaling paradigm: propose many candidate solutions and keep those that are dynamically stable, which alone reaches high solve rates on Sudoku-Shah (~$100\%$) and Zebra ($95.9\%$). This even generalizes to harder out-of-distribution puzzles like Sudoku-Extreme ($96.1\%$), without ever training on that distribution. This pure search, however, wastes a great deal of computation generating incorrect candidate solutions. We therefore design a training recipe to improve the base model's efficiency. First, we train flow models with a self-conditioning channel and close it at inference, letting them refine their own past predictions. Second, we train models to avoid their own failed generations using direct preference optimization. These changes substantially improve the base model's efficiency, letting it reach $99.2\%$ on Sudoku in just $7$ forward passes, over $8\times$ fewer than the strongest matched masked-diffusion baseline we compare needs for the same accuracy. When combined with test-time scaling, this lets flow models solve hard out-of-distribution puzzles (e.g. Sudoku-Extreme) far more efficiently.

</details>


### [25] [On design-unbiased algorithmic Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28795)
*Li-Chun Zhang,Siu-Ming Tam,Luis Sanguiao-Sande,Wesley Yung,Anders Holmberg*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 本文研究了在有限总体中，机器学习算法（如kNN、随机森林）在无偏预测或分类的条件，包括训练数据采样、算法调优和性能评估，基于已知抽样设计而非假设分布或模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习算法追求预测性能（如最小化MSE或F-score），但无法直接保证无偏性，而在官方统计等场景中，无偏性至关重要。因此需要探究算法无偏的条件。

Method: 基于已知的概率抽样设计（样本和训练集的抽取方式），而非假设的分布或模型，分析如何从有限总体中采样训练数据、调整算法以实现无偏预测或分类，以及如何无偏评估样本外预测性能。

Result: 明确了在给定有限总体下，通过概率抽样和算法调优可实现无偏预测或分类的条件，并提出了无偏评估样本外性能的方法。

Conclusion: 利用概率抽样设计而非模型假设，能够导出机器学习在有限总体中的无偏推断框架，为官方统计等需要无偏性的应用提供理论基础。

Abstract: Machine Learning (ML) algorithms, such as k-Nearest Neighbours (kNN) or random forest, eschew the ideal of true data models in favour of predictive performance. However, minimising the MSE or F-score cannot lead to unbiasedness directly, which is important in many situations such as official statistics. We study the conditions of algorithmic ML, other than the existence and knowledge of true data models, which lead to unbiased prediction or classification for a given finite population, including how the training data may be sampled from the population, how a trained prediction algorithm can be tuned to achieve unbiased prediction or classification for that population, and how the performance of out-of-sample prediction or classification can be assessed unbiasedly. The inference is based on the known probability design of samples and training sets, rather than any assumed distributions or models.

</details>


### [26] [HARD-KV: Head-Adaptive Regularization for Decoding-time KV Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28831)
*Yuxuan Yang,Feiyang Ren,Bowen Zeng,Dalin Zhang,Jinpeng Chen,Gang Chen,Huan Li*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出了HARD-KV框架，通过Cascade Cache层级和Logits Calibration机制解决长上下文LLM推理中动态压缩与静态内存管理的冲突，实现高达2倍吞吐量提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文LLM推理中，头部自适应压缩算法（如Top-p采样）精度高但内存动态变化，而现代推理引擎（如vLLM）要求静态内存模式以利用CUDA Graphs和PagedAttention，存在‘静态-动态’不匹配问题。

Method: 设计Cascade Cache层级（密集、稀疏、压缩三层）管理token生命周期；提出Logits Calibration机制，将不同重要性指标归一化为统一概率空间，实现跨异质头的Top-p预算分配；通过系统级方案将碎片化动态索引重写为连续物理布局。

Result: 在数学推理基准（AIME, U-Math）上，10k+ token场景中相比静态基线实现高达2倍吞吐量提升，同时保持高保真生成。

Conclusion: HARD-KV有效统一了动态压缩与静态系统约束，在不牺牲精度前提下显著提升长上下文推理效率。

Abstract: Long-context LLM inference faces a fundamental conflict: head-adaptive compression algorithms (e.g., Top-$p$ nucleus sampling) offer superior accuracy by dynamically fluctuating memory budgets, yet modern inference engines (e.g., vLLM) demand rigid, static memory patterns to leverage CUDA Graphs and PagedAttention. We resolve this ``Static-Dynamic'' mismatch with HARD-KV, a unified framework that that bridges dynamic selection with rigid system constraints. HARD-KV introduces a Cascade Cache hierarchy, managing the token lifecycle across dense, sparse, and condensed tiers. Crucially, we propose a Logits Calibration mechanism that normalizes diverse importance metrics into a unified probability space, enabling consistent Top-$p$ budgeting across heterogeneous heads. To bridge the efficiency gap, we offer a system-level solution, which rewrites fragmented, dynamic indices into contiguous physical layouts compatible with high-performance inference engine. Extensive experiments on math-reasoning benchmarks (AIME, U-Math) verify that HARD-KV achieves up to 2$\times$ throughput improvement over static baselines while maintaining high-fidelity generation in 10k+ token scenarios. Code is available at https://github.com/SuDIS-ZJU/HARDInfer.

</details>


### [27] [Selective Memory Retention for Long-Horizon LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29178)
*Pranath Reddy*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 记忆增强在无噪声环境下提升LLM智能体性能，但不同保留策略差异不显著；在噪声干扰下，TraceRetain-CEM保持性能，而无限内存和FIFO-K50下降；有界保留策略在无噪声时节省内存且不影响任务成功率，仅在噪声流中优于缓存启发式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究外部边界记忆在冻结LLM智能体中的作用，特别是在不同保留策略下何时影响性能，以及内存污染问题在清洁和噪声环境中的表现。

Method: 提出TraceRetain框架，通过可解释特征（成功、年龄、访问频率、冗余、特异性、相似性、下游效用）对条目评分，容量满时驱逐最低分条目，在ALFWorld环境使用gpt-5-mini进行实验。

Result: 清洁环境下有记忆优于无记忆，但不同边界策略差异不显著（Wilson 95% CIs）；噪声应激下，无限内存和FIFO-K50的Precision@5下降（20.2%→12.4%，15.8%→3.8%），TraceRetain-CEM几乎不变（16.9%→16.6%）并保持97/100任务成功；无限内存相似度最高（0.87）但精度最低。

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: When does retention matter for memory-augmented LLM agents? We study this with TraceRetain, a lightweight framework for bounded external memory in frozen LLM agents that scores entries by interpretable features (success, age, access frequency, redundancy, specificity, similarity, downstream utility) and evicts the lowest-scoring ones at capacity. On clean ALFWorld with gpt-5-mini, external memory robustly improves over no memory across two seeds, but differences among bounded retention policies fall within Wilson 95% CIs: clean ALFWorld at T=100 to T=200 does not naturally exhibit the memory pollution retention is designed to address. Under a controlled noisy-write stress (75% synthetic distractors), unbounded memory and FIFO-K50 degrade on Precision@5 (20.2% to 12.4% and 15.8% to 3.8%) while TraceRetain-CEM is essentially unchanged (16.9% to 16.6%) and preserves 97/100 task success. The mechanism: unbounded memory has the highest mean similarity (0.87) but lowest precision, indicating failed distractors close to the query in embedding space. Held-out in-distribution evaluation shows memory-augmented policies solving 47 to 49 of 50 tasks vs. 39/50 for no memory. Bounded retention buys memory and step efficiency on saturated clean benchmarks at no task-success cost, and only differentiates from cache heuristics when streams contain noise.

</details>


### [28] [Evidence-Informed LLM Beliefs for Continual Scientific Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29182)
*Dhruv Agarwal,Reece Adamson,Andrew McCallum,Peter Clark,Ashish Sabharwal,Bodhisattwa Prasad Majumder*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出基于非平稳惊讶度量的持续科学发现方法，通过证据更新信念并优化搜索策略，避免虚假奖励，提升发现多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法如AutoDiscovery将惊讶度视为静态量，不符合人类推理中信念随经验演变的非平稳特性，导致无法持续发现。

Method: 采用嵌入检索增强生成更新先验信念，计算非平稳惊讶度，并引入信念更新过滤与多样性最大化两个搜索改进。

Result: 在五个领域，非平稳惊讶度平均提升30.62%，识别出37.5%的静态惊讶度为虚假。

Conclusion: 持续科学发现需要非平稳信念测量与避免冗余、鼓励多样性的搜索策略相结合。

Abstract: Open-ended scientific discovery with large language models (LLMs) increasingly operates as a long-horizon loop of hypothesis search and verification, where a reward signal guides which hypotheses to test next. A notable recent example is AutoDiscovery, which uses "Bayesian surprise" - the belief shift an LLM undergoes after observing evidence for a hypothesis - as both a discovery metric and a reward for search. We first observe that AutoDiscovery treats surprisal as a static quantity, while surprisal in human reasoning is non-stationary - it is defined relative to beliefs that evolve with experience, a prerequisite for continual scientific discovery. We address this mismatch with evidence-informed LLM beliefs: priors updated with evidence from previous hypotheses to compute non-stationary surprisal for new hypotheses. We compare in-context belief-updating mechanisms and find that embedding-based retrieval-augmented generation over prior discoveries best anticipates eventual posteriors, identifying 37.5% of static surprisals as spurious. We then modify search to avoid these spurious rewards and prioritize hypotheses that remain surprising under non-stationary beliefs. Concretely, we introduce two complementary changes to the original search procedure: belief-update filtering and diversity maximization. Across five discovery domains, our method increases accumulated non-stationary surprisal by 30.62% on average compared to the original search procedure, demonstrating that continual scientific discovery with LLMs requires not only better belief measurement but also search procedures that avoid redundancy and encourage diversity.

</details>


### [29] [AI Trading's Alpha Singularity: Emergent Market Reasoning through Agent-to-Agent Self-Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29194)
*Yuqi Li,Siyuan Liu,Bingjun Liu*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出密封联合搜索框架，通过结构条件防止过拟合，在CSI1000上实现夏普比率1.87。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统α挖掘固定评分函数导致搜索过拟合，需将评分函数作为搜索构件联合优化。

Method: 设计密封联合搜索框架，包含角色分解、类型化通信、溯源密封读取等条件，通过Agora系统实现五类LLM代理协作。

Result: 在91天2026年未参与数据集上夏普比率1.87，超基线最高1.33，消融实验验证框架有效性。

Conclusion: 密封联合搜索可防止自我确认，使评价指标自发涌现而非预设，但存在单次运行和信号集中等局限。

Abstract: Automated alpha mining holds the scoring function fixed and varies the search algorithm over it. A search that converges against a fixed scorer overfits whatever the scorer cannot penalize, a primary cause of the out-of-sample generalization gap. We treat the scoring function as a search artifact alongside the alpha factors and study what conditions make this joint search admissible. Sealed Joint Search (SJS) is a framework: a set of structural conditions on information flow in an autonomous-discovery system that prevent joint search from collapsing into self-confirmation while keeping the evaluator sealed. Conditions cover role decomposition, typed inter-role communication, provenance-sealed reads, versioned stores, and substrate-local promotion. Agora tests SJS empirically: five LLM agent classes communicate via three channels, evolving eight skill libraries, with alpha libraries built on AlphaGen operators. Three evaluators write reports aggregated into one brief, carrying forward disagreement instead of voting. We run Agora for 100 rounds on CSI 1000 and evaluate on a 91-day 2026 holdout sealed from all LLM inputs. Agora achieves holdout Sharpe +1.87; best baseline +1.334 at favorable seed and -0.755 cross-seed mean. Pre-loading Agora's two metrics into a frozen-library ablation recovers only +0.40 of the +2.25 Sharpe gap, and adding PPO without library evolution worsens the gap. The two metrics emerge rather than being designed. Caveats: single-seed run, short-side concentrated signal, intended for long-short.

</details>


### [30] [MALOQ: Massively Accelerated Learning of Operators for Quantum Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28911)
*Manasa Kaniselvan,Alexander Maeder,Denghui Lu,Alexandros Nikolaos Ziogas,Mathieu Luisier*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出MALOQ，一种基于SO(2)-等变架构的机器学习框架，用于高效预测大规模体系的DFT哈密顿量/密度矩阵，支持从几个到10万原子的系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统DFT计算成本高，难以处理大体系；现有ML算子模型扩展性不足，无法高效处理高秩哈密顿矩阵和大规模原子图。

Method: 采用SO(2)-等变骨干架构，设计自定义数据处理内核处理高秩矩阵，并实现原子图的边级分布式计算。
